Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on clusters structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 498. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 660 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 498 + O(1/n^660). Summing over all modes and applying the clusters convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
